# Profile addresses fresh (`/profile/addresses`)

Source: logged-in live site `https://ecommerce-route-ashen.vercel.app/profile/addresses`, captured at 1920 / 1024 / 390. Test email redacted. Cart/checkout/wishlist have no `<main>`; the saved content root is the page wrapper. Raw markup: `profile-addresses-fresh.html`.
